# Commands · A · part 12

1,306 entries, audit-bounded-context to auto. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **audit-bounded-context**: Audit a repo for DDD bounded-context boundary leakage — a module owned by one context importing another context's internals instead of going through a shared/published interface (Conway's-Law drift), plus domain purity at the context seam and ubiquitous-language drift. Emits a findings doc, an OpenSpec remediation change, Bilingual Linear issues, and 4-cure scaffold proposals. Accepts a target path as $ARGUMENTS. ([chimeranext/better-toolkits](https://github.com/chimeranext/better-toolkits/blob/HEAD/toolkits/make-no-mistakes-toolkit/commands/audit-bounded-context.md) · ★4)
- **audit-brand-usage**: Audit the web app for raw-color / library-first violations (report only, don't fix) ([mreimitz/elabs-ai-workbench](https://github.com/mreimitz/elabs-ai-workbench/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/audit-brand-usage.md) · ★4)
- **audit-brief**: 감사자에게 줄 브리핑(기준 커밋과의 diff · 변경 파일 · 커밋 범위)을 만든다. 감사자는 Read · Grep · Glob 만 가져 과거와의 비교가 전부 확인불가로 남습니다. **사용자가 PR · 브랜치 · 커밋 범위나 기준을 말했을 때만** 쓰세요. 지금 코드를 봐 달라는 요청이면 이것 없이 감사자를 바로 부르세요 — 기준 없이 부르면 되묻고 멈춥니다. ([pdw96/claude-kit](https://github.com/pdw96/claude-kit/blob/HEAD/vibe-audit/commands/audit-brief.md))
- **audit-build**: Pipeline stage after /implement-trd. Runs the delivered-code audit wave against a TRD and its PRD (traceability is the headline check), writes the audit report, and chains /implement-trd --reconcile for tasks found unbuilt unless --report-only. A passing audit closes the feature. ([FortiumPartners/ensemble-vnext](https://github.com/FortiumPartners/ensemble-vnext/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/audit-build.md) · ★1 · 2 copies)
- **audit-bundle**: Walk every claim the bundle makes about the Lancet2 source code, compare against the actual source, and report drift. This is the source-of-truth refresh ritual that complements `/sync-cost-model` (which refreshes against Anthropic's external docs); this command refreshes against the project's own source files. ([nygenome/Lancet2](https://github.com/nygenome/Lancet2/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/audit-bundle.md) · ★29)
- **audit-bundle-network**: Audit a Databricks VPC for missing PrivateLink endpoints that leak NAT cost ([jeremylongshore/tons-of-skills-marketplace](https://github.com/jeremylongshore/tons-of-skills-marketplace/blob/HEAD/plugins/saas-packs/databricks-pack/skills/databricks-bundle-medic/commands/audit-bundle-network.md) · ★2.9k · 3 copies)
- **audit-cache**: Codebase-wide cache audit — barcha @Cacheable annotation'larni cache-strategist agent orqali tekshirish ([FeruzLatifov/hemis-back](https://github.com/FeruzLatifov/hemis-back/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/audit-cache.md))
- **audit-candidate**: Walk the highest-signal suspicious candidate through an implementation audit. ([ProjectDXAI/labrat](https://github.com/ProjectDXAI/labrat/blob/HEAD/examples/nlp-sentiment/research_lab/.claude/commands/audit-candidate.md) · ★222 · 2 copies)
- **audit-canonicalisation**: Audit canonical form and semantic identity with task-specific source and runtime evidence. ([tachyon-beep/skillpacks](https://github.com/tachyon-beep/skillpacks/blob/HEAD/plugins/yzmir-structure-synthesis/commands/audit-canonicalisation.md) · ★15)
- **audit-case-escalation**: &gt; **Deprecation notice:** this command is now an alias. It invokes &gt; [`audit-router`](../agents/audit-router/AGENT.md) with &gt; `--domain=case_escalation` and emits this deprecation notice. Switch to &gt; `/audit-router` at your convenience; the alias ships until the removal &gt; window declared in `docs/MIGRATION.md` (Wave 7). ([PranavNagrecha/AwesomeSalesforceSkills](https://github.com/PranavNagrecha/AwesomeSalesforceSkills/blob/HEAD/commands/audit-case-escalation.md) · ★26 · 2 copies)
- **audit-case-file.prompt**: Audit a case file and its unsent drafts for evidentiary integrity — verify every checkable assertion against a primary source, classify findings by severity, and report without editing anything. ([raandree/CopilotAtelier](https://github.com/raandree/CopilotAtelier/blob/HEAD/com.github.copilot/commands/audit-case-file.prompt.md) · ★19)
- **audit-catalog**: Run a SYSTEMATIC, exhaustive audit of `.docs/common-issues/` (default) — or `.docs/principles/` if `$ARGUMENTS` says "principles" (or "both"). Cross-session catalogs accrete overlap, broken links, and doc↔code drift that incremental passes miss; this finds them in one pass. Common-issues need this more often than principles (raw incidents vs distilled-via-how-to-learn). ([tomdwipo/claude-soul](https://github.com/tomdwipo/claude-soul/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/audit-catalog.md) · ★24)
- **audit-cc**: Run cyclomatic complexity audit and block push if any D/E/F-grade functions exist. ([RamanaAmbore/algo-trader](https://github.com/RamanaAmbore/algo-trader/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/audit-cc.md) · ★3)
- **audit-cd**: Check publish drift across destination registries — npm, PyPI, Open VSX, VS Marketplace, AMO (Firefox), GitHub Releases — via public read APIs. Read-only, makes outbound HTTPS. ([starter-series/create-starter](https://github.com/starter-series/create-starter/blob/HEAD/.claude-plugin/commands/audit-cd.md) · ★2)
- **audit-chain**: Verify every receipt in ./receipts/receipts.jsonl against the signer's public key. Detects tampered or malformed receipts across the audit trail. ([wshobson/agents](https://github.com/wshobson/agents/blob/HEAD/plugins/protect-mcp/commands/audit-chain.md) · ★43k · 4 copies)
- **audit-chain**: Walk the receipt chain in ./receipts/ verifying every signature and hash link. Detects insertions, deletions, and tampering across the entire audit trail. ([mtecoYY/ai-baike](https://github.com/mtecoYY/ai-baike/blob/HEAD/%E7%A0%B4%E7%94%B2/Codex-X/skills-web/agents/plugins/protect-mcp/commands/audit-chain.md) · ★41 · 21 copies)
- **audit-chain**: Verify every receipt and chain link in ./receipts/receipts.jsonl against the signer's public key. Detects tampered, malformed, deleted, or reordered receipts. ([shotcowboystyle/ag-skills](https://github.com/shotcowboystyle/ag-skills/blob/HEAD/plugins/protect-mcp/commands/audit-chain.md))

- **auth-lark**: Authenticate Lark (Feishu) in BOTH token modes and verify permissions — tenant (app token from LARK_APP_ID/LARK_APP_SECRET) and/or user (UAT via OAuth login). Probes what each mode can do (Bitable / Drive / Docs), resolves the effective read mode (preference user(default)|auto|tenant; user prefers the real-person token so writes are traceable, auto picks the mode with more granted read scopes tie→tenant), writes structured lark info to lark-auth.state.json + env, and reports any conflict with what a command needs. You (Claude) run scripts/lark_auth.py yourself; the user runs no terminal comma… ([hominhtuong/qa-claude-plugins](https://github.com/hominhtuong/qa-claude-plugins/blob/HEAD/plugins/qa/commands/auth-lark.md) · ★2)
- **auth-login**: How an account is signed in: gws-axi prepares an OAuth request (PKCE, loopback redirect to `http://127.0.0.1:&lt;port&gt;/callback`), the user approves it in a browser, and gws-axi exchanges the returned code for tokens. The flow is split so an agent can relay instructions before anything blocks: `auth login --no-wait` prepares, `auth login --wait` listens. ([JarvusInnovations/gws-axi](https://github.com/JarvusInnovations/gws-axi/blob/HEAD/specs/commands/auth-login.md) · ★10)
